**C. Plant cell-signaling peptide (PSK/PSY-like)**
- Phytosulfokines (PSKs) and Plantacyanin/PSY peptides are plant signaling peptides
- PSKs are small sulfated pentapeptides derived from larger precursors with signal peptides
- PSY peptides (Plant Peptide Growth Regulators) are small, cysteine-rich peptides with signal peptides

- PSK precursors have: signal peptide + pro-peptide region + mature PSK (sulfated Tyr-Tyr-Gln-Xaa-Gly-OH)

- These are involved in cell signaling, stress responses, growth and development

- The cysteine-rich nature and the overall architecture (signal peptide + small mature peptide) strongly suggests a plant signaling peptide

The architecture of a hydrophobic signal peptide followed by a cysteine-rich mature peptide region is characteristic of plant signaling peptides like PSK/PSY-type molecules.
